\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints()}{remove\_dynamic\_constraints()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [1/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{std\+::list$<$ Const $>$ \&}]{list,  }\item[{std\+::vector$<$ typename std\+::list$<$ Const $>$\+::const\+\_\+iterator $>$ \&}]{rmvd,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_ga544246d8c462e9a2d817c2c30797968f}{c\+\_\+\+Mod\+Param}}}]{issue\+Mod = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_gga48fddcdd03a03f49b7e389ca1ed938d4a21b14b26b1c0093a0905f6fc010bad29}{e\+Mod\+Blck}}} }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



removes a bunch of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} from the given list 

Removes a bunch of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} from the given list.

The parameter list is obviously not \char`\"{}const\char`\"{}, as the list will be updated. Note that the base class implementation of this method just does this and possibly issues the appropriate \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}}; as list is supposed to be a part of the \char`\"{}abstract representation\char`\"{}, this means that the \char`\"{}physical representation\char`\"{} of the corresponding dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} (if any exists) is not updated, as this should clearly be responsibility of the derived class (maybe within the corresponding implementation of this method).

The parameter issue\+Mod decides if and how the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} is issued, as described in \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_observer_a2d499ae38e5e95cf66cd71da32b0694a}{Observer\+::make\+\_\+par()}}.

The removed \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} are these whose (const) iterator is found in the std\+::vector rmvd. Note that, if the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} is issued, these \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} are not immediately deleted; rather, they are added to a list stored into the boost\+::any field of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} object, so that they remain alive until the last interested \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} had had the chance to use them to perform the required changes. This also implies that the same memory address cannot be used for new \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} (or anything), which also avoids potential problems. As soon as the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} is eventually destroyed, the Constraints are destroyed as well. If, instead, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} is not issed, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} are destroyed immediately.

Important note\+: each \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} knows which are the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} that are active in it. Likewise, each \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} knows which \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} (among other things) it is active in. Therefore, the information about whether a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} is active in a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} can be obtained in two ways\+:

1) asking a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} about whether it is active in a particular \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}};

2) asking a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} about whether a particular \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} is active in it.

When a dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} is removed, any \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} that is still active in this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} at the time this method is called will no longer be active in it. This will be accomplished by removing the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} from each \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} that was active in it. This means that the first source of information about activeness listed above will be updated. However, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} will still keep the information about which \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} were active in it before its removal. This means that the second source of information about activeness will not be updated. This is OK because the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} will no longer exist in the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, and it can actually be useful for the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} to efficiently process the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}}.

Note that when the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} is removed from the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}}, no \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} is issued (see \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable_a2e531111ee80083b60e8b3775ad3ce4f}{Variable\+::remove\+\_\+active()}}); thus, calling this method does not trigger any other \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} apart from the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_acd2feed47c85ecb4cbd6cc4b3f6bbf58}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_acd2feed47c85ecb4cbd6cc4b3f6bbf58}} 
\index{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}!remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints@{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints}}
\index{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints@{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints}!S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}}
\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints()}{remove\_dynamic\_constraints()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [2/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{std\+::list$<$ Const $\ast$$>$ \&}]{list,  }\item[{std\+::vector$<$ typename std\+::list$<$ Const $\ast$$>$\+::const\+\_\+iterator $>$ \&}]{rmvd,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_ga544246d8c462e9a2d817c2c30797968f}{c\+\_\+\+Mod\+Param}}}]{issue\+Mod = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_gga48fddcdd03a03f49b7e389ca1ed938d4a21b14b26b1c0093a0905f6fc010bad29}{e\+Mod\+Blck}}} }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



removes a bunch of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} $\ast$ from the given list 

Removes a bunch of pointers to \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} from the given list.

Same as remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints( std\+::list$<$\+Const$>$ \&list , ... ), except the arguments are lists of pointers rather than lists of objects. For most aspects, see the comments of that method.

The specific difference of this version is that the list of {\itshape pointers} to the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} to be removed is stored in a list into the boost\+::any field of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} object. The list is destroyed when the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} object is, i.\+e., automatically when the last registered \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} has deleted its copy. However, this being a list of {\itshape pointers}, this does {\itshape not} guarantee that the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} themselves will be destroyed, nor that they will {\itshape not} be destroyed too early (before all \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} are done with them). Ensuring this remains on the shoulders of whomever had created them in the first place. This should be doable, because a list of pointers to \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} likely is not the \char`\"{}physical representation\char`\"{} of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}}; hence, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} should \char`\"{}physically\char`\"{} be in some other data structure in the \+:\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, which therefore has to ensure that they will not be deleted too early (or too late).

However, clearly having dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} as lista of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}}, rather than lists of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} $\ast$, looks better. There is one level less of indirection, and the destruction of the object is automatically managed as opposed to requiring specific care. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a1c7a7f0599403293cdb7cecd381329e3}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a1c7a7f0599403293cdb7cecd381329e3}} 
\index{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}!remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables@{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables}}
\index{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables@{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables}!S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{std\+::list$<$ Var $>$ \&}]{list,  }\item[{std\+::vector$<$ typename std\+::list$<$ Var $>$\+::const\+\_\+iterator $>$ \&}]{rmvd,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_ga544246d8c462e9a2d817c2c30797968f}{c\+\_\+\+Mod\+Param}}}]{issue\+Mod = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_gga48fddcdd03a03f49b7e389ca1ed938d4a21b14b26b1c0093a0905f6fc010bad29}{e\+Mod\+Blck}}},  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_ga544246d8c462e9a2d817c2c30797968f}{c\+\_\+\+Mod\+Param}}}]{issueind\+Mod = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_gga48fddcdd03a03f49b7e389ca1ed938d4a21b14b26b1c0093a0905f6fc010bad29}{e\+Mod\+Blck}}} }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



removes a bunch of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} from the given list 

Removes a bunch of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} from the given list.

The parameter list is obviously not \char`\"{}const\char`\"{}, as the list will be updated. Note that the base class implementation of this method just does this and possibly issues the appropriate \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}}; as list is supposed to be a part of the \char`\"{}abstract representation\char`\"{}, this means that the \char`\"{}physical
representation\char`\"{} of the corresponding dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} (if any exists) is not updated, as this should clearly be responsibility of the derived class (maybe within the corresponding implementation of this method).

The parameter issue\+Mod decides if and how the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} is issued, as described in \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_observer_a2d499ae38e5e95cf66cd71da32b0694a}{Observer\+::make\+\_\+par()}}.

The removed \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} are these whose (const) iterator is found in the std\+::vector rmvd. Note that, if the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} is issued, these \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} are not immediately deleted; rather, they are added to a list stored into the boost\+::any field of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} object, so that they remain alive until the last interested \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} had had the chance to use them to perform the required changes. This also implies that the same memory address cannot be used for new \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} (or anything), which also avoids potential problems. As soon as the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} is eventually destroyed, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} are destroyed as well. If, instead, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} is not issed, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} are destroyed immediately.

Important note\+: each \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} knows which are the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} that are active in it. Likewise, each \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} knows which \char`\"{}stuff\char`\"{} (\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_objective}{Objective}}, ...) it is active in. Therefore, the information about whether a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} is active in a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} can be obtained in two ways\+:

1) asking a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} about whether it is active in a particular \char`\"{}stuff\char`\"{};

2) Asking a \char`\"{}stuff\char`\"{} about whether a particular \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} is active in it.

When a dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} is removed from a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, it will no longer be active in any \char`\"{}stuff\char`\"{}. This will be accomplished by removing the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} from each \char`\"{}stuff\char`\"{} in which it is active. This means that the second source of information about activeness listed above will be updated. However, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} will still keep the information about which \char`\"{}stuff\char`\"{} it was active in before its removal. This means that the first source of information about activeness will not be updated. This is OK because the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} will no longer exist in the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, and it can actually be useful for the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} to efficiently process the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}}.

Yet, unlike removing a dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} (see the comments to \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a70a454edfce317205e2b0e65823a8322}{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+constraints()}}), removing a dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} from a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} is a complex task. Besides this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} issuing a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod_a_d}{Block\+Mod\+AD}} stating that this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} has been removed, other \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} are in principle issued. For each \char`\"{}stuff\char`\"{} in which the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} is active, \mbox{\hyperlink{group___thin_var_dep_interface___c_l_a_s_s_e_s_gad6726141a0cbe6a0410a2b396809dade}{Thin\+Var\+Dep\+Interface\+::remove\+\_\+variable()}} will be called, and this in principle issues a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} on its own. The further parameter issueind\+Mod is provided to control this, with the usual format described in \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_observer_a2d499ae38e5e95cf66cd71da32b0694a}{Observer\+::make\+\_\+par()}}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} are crucial to maintaining the coherence between the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} and the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}}\+: hence, choosing to disable them should be a very well thought out decision. However, avoiding issuing the individual Modifications may be useful (more efficient) in some cases. For instance, a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} may have a huge number of {\itshape linear} \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}}, and a dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} may be active in many of those. If this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} is removed from the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, it will be removed from each \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} in which is active, thereby triggering a large number of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} being issued. However, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} attached to the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} may be able to deal with the removal of this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} without relying on the individual \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}}. Indeed, the change in all the linear constraints due to such a removal are trivial, and possibly handled with a single simple operation (say, removing one column of the coefficients matrix). We stress, however, that setting issueind\+Mod to e\+No\+Mod must be a completely conscious decision. If one is not sure whether the individual \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} are needed, this parameter should be left to the default value (or to e\+No\+Blck when appropriate). \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a176ce3ef1f4a8c33e242acb066482d45}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a176ce3ef1f4a8c33e242acb066482d45}} 
\index{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}!remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables@{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables}}
\index{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables@{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables}!S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}}
\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables()}{remove\_dynamic\_variables()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [2/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{std\+::list$<$ Var $\ast$$>$ \&}]{list,  }\item[{std\+::vector$<$ typename std\+::list$<$ Var $\ast$$>$\+::const\+\_\+iterator $>$ \&}]{rmvd,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_ga544246d8c462e9a2d817c2c30797968f}{c\+\_\+\+Mod\+Param}}}]{issue\+Mod = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_gga48fddcdd03a03f49b7e389ca1ed938d4a21b14b26b1c0093a0905f6fc010bad29}{e\+Mod\+Blck}}},  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_ga544246d8c462e9a2d817c2c30797968f}{c\+\_\+\+Mod\+Param}}}]{issueind\+Mod = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{group___modification___t_y_p_e_s_gga48fddcdd03a03f49b7e389ca1ed938d4a21b14b26b1c0093a0905f6fc010bad29}{e\+Mod\+Blck}}} }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



removes a bunch of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} $\ast$ from the given list 

Removes a bunch of pointers to \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} from the given list.

Same as remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables( std\+::list$<$\+Var$>$ \&list , ... ), except the arguments are lists of pointers rather than lists of objects. For most aspects, see the comments of that method.

The specific difference of this version is that the list of {\itshape pointers} to the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} to be removed is stored in a list into the boost\+::any field of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} object. The list is destroyed when the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} object is, i.\+e., automatically when the last registered \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} has deleted its copy. However, this being a list of {\itshape pointers}, this does {\itshape not} guarantee that the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} themselves will be destroyed, nor that they will {\itshape not} be destroyed too early (before all \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} are done with them). Ensuring this remains on the shoulders of whomever had created them in the first place. This should be doable because a list of pointers to \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} likely is not the \char`\"{}physical representation\char`\"{} of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}}; hence, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} should \char`\"{}physically\char`\"{} be in some other data structure in the \+:\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, which therefore has to ensure that they will not be deleted too early (or too late).

However, clearly having dynamic \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} as lista of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}}, rather than lists of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} $\ast$, looks better. There is one level less of indirection, and the destruction of the object is automatically managed as opposed to requiring specific care. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a164c030eea2f1b177de50db119a63a32}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a164c030eea2f1b177de50db119a63a32}} 
\index{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}!remove\+\_\+variable\+\_\+from\+\_\+stuff@{remove\+\_\+variable\+\_\+from\+\_\+stuff}}
\index{remove\+\_\+variable\+\_\+from\+\_\+stuff@{remove\+\_\+variable\+\_\+from\+\_\+stuff}!S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void remove\+\_\+variable\+\_\+from\+\_\+stuff (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} $\ast$const}]{variable,  }\item[{const int}]{issueind\+Mod }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [private]}}

This method removes the given \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} from all Constraints and Objectives in which it is active. Since the removal of a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_variable}{Variable}} from a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_constraint}{Constraint}} or \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_objective}{Objective}} may result in a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} being thrown, this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} can be avoided by setting the parameter throw\+\_\+individual\+\_\+modifications to false \mbox{[}see \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a1c7a7f0599403293cdb7cecd381329e3}{remove\+\_\+dynamic\+\_\+variables()}} above\mbox{]}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_ae7306554741d39d82838487ae623daed}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_ae7306554741d39d82838487ae623daed}}

replace an old \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} with a new \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} 

Method for substituting the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} at the position \char`\"{}it\char`\"{} into the vector returned by \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a052e250efe81b0840361e70c520bdd7f}{get\+\_\+registered\+\_\+solvers()}} \mbox{[}see above\mbox{]} with the given new \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}}. Note that the iterator parameter is const because the only place where it might have been taken from (except if the method is called from another \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} method) is \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a052e250efe81b0840361e70c520bdd7f}{get\+\_\+registered\+\_\+solvers()}}; however, we \char`\"{}cast away
const-\/ness\char`\"{} inside. Note that the method is virtual because derived classes may have to do more.

Warning\+: checking if an iterator really belongs to a list is complicated, hence the method does not try to do that; clearly, calling the method with something that is not an iterator of that list results in indefinite behaviour. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a8209df4072912666c1ea5f6979c035fc}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a8209df4072912666c1ea5f6979c035fc}}

serialize a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (recursively) to a net\+C\+DF file given the filename 

Top-\/level method to serialize a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (recursively) to a file in net\+C\+D\+F-\/based S\+M\+S++-\/format, given the filename and its type. See deserialize( net\+C\+D\+F\+::\+Nc\+File \&\& ) for details of the different file types. Note that any existing content of the file is overwritten, and that the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} is saved as {\itshape the first one} in the newly created file.

The base class implementation opens the net\+C\+DF file, creates the required attribute \char`\"{}\+S\+M\+S++\+\_\+file\+\_\+type\char`\"{}, assigns it the type, and dispatches to the net\+C\+D\+F\+::\+Nc\+File \&\& version of the method. If anything goes wrong with any step of the process, exception is thrown. Although the method is virtual, it is not expected that derived classes will have a need to re-\/define it. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a7f0d5cf07b22aef912bfa8ac4b45924c}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a7f0d5cf07b22aef912bfa8ac4b45924c}}

\index{serialize@{serialize}!S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}}
\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{serialize()}{serialize()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [2/3]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily virtual void serialize (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{net\+C\+D\+F\+::\+Nc\+File \&\&}]{f,  }\item[{const int}]{type }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [virtual]}}



serialize a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (recursively) to an open net\+C\+DF file 

Second-\/level method to serialize a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (recursively) to an open net\+C\+DF file in net\+C\+D\+F-\/based S\+M\+S++-\/format. The type of the file, provided as a parameter (mainly to make the signature of the method not ambiguous with the serialize( net\+C\+D\+F\+::\+Nc\+Group ) one), must be the same as that found in the \+:S\+M\+S++\+\_\+file\+\_\+type" attribute, with the meaning set out by the enum smspp\+\_\+net\+C\+D\+F\+\_\+file\+\_\+type \mbox{[}see \mbox{\hyperlink{_s_m_s_typedefs_8h}{S\+M\+S\+Typedefs.\+h}}\mbox{]}.

The current \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} is {\itshape appended} after any existing \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} in the file.

The base class implementation creates the new group (and, if necessary, child group) in the file and dispatches to serialize( net\+C\+D\+F\+::\+Nc\+Group ), which is where the \+:Block-\/dependent serialization happens. If anything goes wrong with any step of the process, exception is thrown. Although the method is virtual, it is not expected that derived classes will have a need to re-\/define it. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a59f3f59d72c56028f06c555bdcd36caf}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a59f3f59d72c56028f06c555bdcd36caf}} 
\index{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}!serialize@{serialize}}
\index{serialize@{serialize}!S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}}
\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{serialize()}{serialize()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [3/3]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily virtual void serialize (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{net\+C\+D\+F\+::\+Nc\+Group \&\&}]{group }\end{DoxyParamCaption}) const\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [virtual]}}



serialize a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (recursively) to a net\+C\+DF Nc\+Group 

Third, and final, level to serialize (recursively) to a net\+C\+DF Nc\+Group. \begin{DoxyVerb}THIS IS THE METHOD TO BE IMPLEMENTED BY DERIVED CLASSES
\end{DoxyVerb}


All the information required to de-\/serialize the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} need be saved in the provided net\+C\+DF Nc\+Group, which is assumed to be \char`\"{}empty\char`\"{}, starting with the \char`\"{}type\char`\"{} attribute that has to contain the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a738e9dfd6e00507da219e4bd5a3f6be9}{name()}} of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. Although each \+:\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} is completely free to organize the net\+C\+DF Nc\+Group as it best sees fit, the idea is that sub-\/\+Blocks (if any) should be saved into child groups of the current group. The idea is that this should be done in pre-\/order\+: the father \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} will create the child groups for each of its sons, and then call the sub-\/\+Block\textquotesingle{}s serialize method with the right Nc\+Group argument. This means that the sub-\/\+Block can rely on dimensions, variables and attributes to have been declared at surrounding scope (in the parent Nc\+Group) before they are called.

An important note applies to \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a8209df4072912666c1ea5f6979c035fc}{serialize()}}\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item A\+NY \+:\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} IS S\+E\+R\+I\+A\+L\+I\+Z\+ED \char`\"{}\+N\+A\+K\+E\+D\char`\"{}

This means that the minimum amount of information required to fully reconstruct it should be saved; typically this is the \char`\"{}physical
  representation\char`\"{} of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, if any exists, maybe with the smallest possible amount of information about the \char`\"{}abstract representation\char`\"{} that is strictly required. As a consequence, nothing of the configuration of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} and the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}}) is saved when the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} is serialized, even less so the attached solvers. The rationale is that \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_configuration}{Configuration}} objects can themselves be serialized, so if saving their current state is required this can (and it is better) done separately.
\end{DoxyItemize}

The method of the base class just creates and fills the \char`\"{}name\char`\"{} attribute (with the right name, thanks to the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a738e9dfd6e00507da219e4bd5a3f6be9}{name()}} method) and then proceeds at saving all its sub-\/\+Block in newly created child Nc\+Group with names \char`\"{}sub-\/\+Block\+\_\+0\char`\"{}, \char`\"{}sub-\/\+Block\+\_\+1\char`\"{}, ... where the index of the sub-\/\+Block is its position in the array returned by \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a39bc932e28075740f08b392830a67e03}{get\+\_\+nested\+\_\+\+Blocks()}}. This can be used by actual \+:\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} if they are OK with the order of construction of the sub-\/\+Block {\itshape and} the vector returned by \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a39bc932e28075740f08b392830a67e03}{get\+\_\+nested\+\_\+\+Blocks()}}, i.\+e., the \char`\"{}abstract representation\char`\"{} of the sub-\/\+Block, is also the \char`\"{}physical one\char`\"{} (i.\+e., there is no distinct \char`\"{}physical representation\char`\"{} of the sub-\/\+Block). If this is done, possibly Block\+::serialize( net\+C\+D\+F\+::\+Nc\+Group ) should be called {\itshape after} the father \+:\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} has already loaded all its information into the (\char`\"{}father\char`\"{}) Nc\+Group, if the sub-\/\+Block are going to use it (possibly reaching the \char`\"{}father\char`\"{} Nc\+Group via get\+Parent\+Group()). At any rate, the implementation of Block\+::serialize( net\+C\+D\+F\+::\+Nc\+Group ) provides a clean blueprint of how this can be done by specific \+:\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. 

Reimplemented in \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_m_c_f_block_a1b39d46ae5259d58a1a1ed05af6c344d}{M\+C\+F\+Block}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_simple_m_i_l_p_block_aefb3f33afdab2b6f4aef3f926c10df51}{Simple\+M\+I\+L\+P\+Block}}, and \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_structured_m_i_l_p_block_aefb3f33afdab2b6f4aef3f926c10df51}{Structured\+M\+I\+L\+P\+Block}}.

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{set\+\_\+\+Block\+Config()}{set\_BlockConfig()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily virtual void set\+\_\+\+Block\+Config (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} $\ast$}]{new\+BC = {\ttfamily nullptr},  }\item[{const bool}]{safe = {\ttfamily true} }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}



setting the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} 

This method sets the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} of this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. The \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} object pointed by new\+BC is not copied, but it becomes property of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. This means that if there already is a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, it gets deleted during the call.

Since a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} has a vector of sub-\/\+Block\+Config for the sub-\/\+Block of this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, it calls itself recursively to set them. Note that this means that there are at typically {\itshape two} \char`\"{}live\char`\"{} pointers to the same \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}}, one in the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} itself and one in the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} object of its father \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, if any.

Hence, a call to \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a18d411b740a47db00d15083c5c7ef2ba}{set\+\_\+\+Block\+Config()}} (with default = nullptr new\+BC) deletes the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} to this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} and all of its sub-\/\+Block, recursively. Note that the vector of sub-\/\+Block\+Config is allowed to be of different size than the number of sub-\/\+Block; if it is larger any extra \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} is simply ignored, if it shorted then all missing sub-\/\+Block\+Config are treated as nullptr. This means that, for instance, it is possible to set the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} of the father \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} while resetting that of its sub-\/\+Block by just leaving the vector of sub-\/\+Block\+Config empty.

Note, however, that changing (or deleting) a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} is only automatically safe if done top-\/down from the \char`\"{}root\char`\"{} \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (the one having no father \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}), as changing the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} of an inner \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} leaves a dangling pointer in the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} of the father \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. This is the reason for the second parameter of the method. If safe == true, the method can assume that the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_config}{Block\+Config}} (if any) of the father \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (if any) has already been updated. If not, the method will have to climb up (provided there is anywhere to climb) and update it (provided there is anything to update). \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_ae1e61f1d3f7185b6824f02488901a0ad}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_ae1e61f1d3f7185b6824f02488901a0ad}} 
\index{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}!set\+\_\+default\+\_\+channel@{set\+\_\+default\+\_\+channel}}

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{set\+\_\+default\+\_\+channel()}{set\_default\_channel()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily virtual void set\+\_\+default\+\_\+channel (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_observer_aa9ad8de42ffaf90023bfed5c085ad8bc}{c\+\_\+\+Chnl\+Name}}}]{chnl = {\ttfamily 0} }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [override]}, {\ttfamily [virtual]}}



set the \char`\"{}default\char`\"{} channel 

This method allows to \char`\"{}silently redirect\char`\"{} any \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} that is added with add\+\_\+\+Modification( mod , 0 ) to the specified open channel. This mechanism is provided in case the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} is generated by a \char`\"{}complex reaction\char`\"{}, i.\+e., in a method called by a method called by a method ... called by the method that performs the changes whose corresponding \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} should be bunched together (and where, most likely, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_group_modification}{Group\+Modification}} is opened). The point is that to send a particular \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} to a particular channel it is necessary to specify the channel name in \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a9b94536a10555dfb680e015fcb4787a8}{add\+\_\+\+Modification()}}, but it may not always be possible to control what name is used in a method called by a method called by a method ... Thus, this mechanism allows to (temporarily) \char`\"{}silently hijack\char`\"{} the \char`\"{}standard channel\char`\"{} 0. Of course, some care has to be exercised while using this.

If chnl is the name of a currently \char`\"{}open\char`\"{} \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_group_modification}{Group\+Modification}} object, which in particular implies that it is != 0, then all subsequent \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} sent to \char`\"{}channel 0\char`\"{} are redirected to that \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_group_modification}{Group\+Modification}}. This ends if set\+\_\+default\+\_\+channel( 0 ) is called, or if the channel to which 0 has been redirected is closed. Calling the method with chnl not the name of an open channel or zero is an error and should throw exception. 

Implements \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_observer_a47f7642288d3e09ad9c792f86797840d}{Observer}}.

change the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_objective}{Objective}} of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} 

Method to entirely change, in one blow, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_objective}{Objective}} object of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. new\+OF is a pointer to an object of class \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_objective}{Objective}}, or of any derived class, which is stored into a boost\+::any field. Note that, this, being a pointer to a single object, may well be the \char`\"{}physical
representation\char`\"{} of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_objective}{Objective}}, but it is dealt with as being the \char`\"{}abstract representation\char`\"{} in that the base \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} class does not claim ownership of new\+OF, i.\+e., it does not delete it in the destructor\+: whomever produced it in the first place (most likely, the current derived \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} class) must take responsibility for this.

The parameter issue\+Mod decides if and how the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_mod}{Block\+Mod}} is issued, as described in \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_observer_a2d499ae38e5e95cf66cd71da32b0694a}{Observer\+::make\+\_\+par()}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a40a2bbe11fbdf470e474f6638fefa66f}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a40a2bbe11fbdf470e474f6638fefa66f}}

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{set\+\_\+\+Solver\+Config()}{set\_SolverConfig()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily virtual void set\+\_\+\+Solver\+Config (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}} $\ast$}]{svcc = {\ttfamily nullptr} }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}



create and set all the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} attached to this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} (and its sub-\/\+Block) 

Method for creating, configuring and registering all the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} that the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} may need.

The method uses a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}}, that holds\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item a std\+::vector$<$std\+::string$>$ containing the class names of the required \+:\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}};
\item a std\+::vector$<$\+Solver\+Config $\ast$$>$ containing the corresponding algorithmic parameters;
\item a std\+::vector$<$\+Block\+Solver\+Config $\ast$$>$ containing the same information for each of the sub-\/\+Block of this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}};
\end{DoxyItemize}

Note that the vector of Solver\+Config $\ast$ is allowed to be shorter than that of names, in which case all the missing entries are treated as nullptr. It is also allowed to be longer, in which case all the extra entries are ignored.

The \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}} also has a field f\+\_\+diff that indicates whether it has to be interpreted in \char`\"{}differential mode\char`\"{}. The behaviour of this method is the following\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item First, the list of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} registered to this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} is scanned, and for each of them the corresponding elements in the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}} are examined. Then\+: = If f\+\_\+diff == true
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item if the name of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is empty then the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is left there, otherwise the existing solver is un-\/registered and deleted and a new solver is created and registered in that position
\item if the corresponding Solver\+Config $\ast$ is null then nothing is done, otherwise the Solver\+Config $\ast$ is passed to the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} = If f\+\_\+diff == false the esisting solver is un-\/registered and deleted, then
\item if the name of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is empty then the vector of registered \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is shortened by one (any Solver\+Config is ignored)
\item otherwise a new solver is created and registered in that position, and the corresponding Solver\+Config is passed to it. Note that this would seem to not allow completely resetting the configuration of some existing \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} withouth changing it, but this is not true\+: it is sufficient to pass it a Solver\+Config object (hence, not nullptr) which is \char`\"{}empty\char`\"{} (no parameter set) but with its f\+\_\+diff field == false \mbox{[}see Solver\+Config\mbox{]}.
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item After the end of the list of currently registered \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is reached (if ever), the behaviour is instead independent on the value of f\+\_\+diff (adding to nothing is setting). If the name of the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is empty then the entry of the vector is ignored, otherwise a new solver is created and registered in that position (at the end) and the corresponding Solver\+Config is passed to it (unless it is nullptr, because setting a nullptr configuration to a newly minted solver is useless).
\end{DoxyItemize}

After this is done for this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, the method is called recursively on each sub-\/\+Block. Here, again, f\+\_\+diff dictates how \char`\"{}void\char`\"{} information is treated\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item if f\+\_\+diff == true and some \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}} $\ast$ is nullptr, then the corresponding sub-\/\+Block is ignored; if the vector of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}} is shorter than the number of sub-\/\+Block, all the non-\/specified ones are left unchanged;
\item if f\+\_\+diff == false and some \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}} $\ast$ is nullptr, then set\+\_\+\+Solver\+Config( nullptr ) is called for the corresponding sub-\/\+Block; if the vector of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_solver_config}{Block\+Solver\+Config}} is shorter than the number of sub-\/\+Block, set\+\_\+\+Solver\+Config( nullptr ) is called for all the non-\/specified ones.
\end{DoxyItemize}

Since a call to set\+\_\+\+Solver\+Config( nullptr ) would not make sense if the \char`\"{}empty\char`\"{} Solver\+Config were intended in the differential sense (it would do nothing), when svcc == nullptr the field f\+\_\+diff should be interpreted as false. Hence, calling \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a40a2bbe11fbdf470e474f6638fefa66f}{set\+\_\+\+Solver\+Config()}} will unregister and delete all \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} attached to this \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} and to each of its sub-\/\+Block.

Note an important difference between this method and \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a4bf9d1856367faf430d0143a6ee55655}{register\+\_\+\+Solver()}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a1be1159f846ad7809d2d0c5c2311c92e}{unregister\+\_\+\+Solver()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_ae7306554741d39d82838487ae623daed}{replace\+\_\+\+Solver()}} \mbox{[}see above\mbox{]}\+: in the latter the new solver have to be already constructed outside of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, and the ones that get un-\/registered are {\itshape not} deleted, which therefore has to be done by whomever created them in the first place outside the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. In this method, instead, the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} are directly constructed (using the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} factory) inside the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}, and correspondingly each \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} that gets un-\/registered is also immediately deleted. Mixing the two styles of managing \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is therefore tricky and cautions should be exercised. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a8f87b193f9ae85d75ed4a3ab798e6fe0}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a8f87b193f9ae85d75ed4a3ab798e6fe0}}

This method allows to \char`\"{}finalize\char`\"{} the \char`\"{}inner\char`\"{} (...) \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_group_modification}{Group\+Modification}} associated with the given channe, in the sense that and addition of \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_modification}{Modification}} is resumed for the \char`\"{}father\char`\"{} \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_group_modification}{Group\+Modification}} of that object (which is still associated to the same channel).

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{unregister\+\_\+\+Solver()}{unregister\_Solver()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [1/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily virtual void unregister\+\_\+\+Solver (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} $\ast$}]{old\+Solver }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [virtual]}}



removing old\+Solver from the set of those currently registered 

Method for removing a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} from the set of those currently registered with the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. If old\+Solver is not among the registered solvers, then nothing is done (and no warning is issued); otherwise the vector of registered \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is shortened by one, and the remaining solvers (if any) are shifted in the obvious way. Note that the method is virtual because derived classes may have to do more. \mbox{\Hypertarget{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a5cd5238acd84dd259f3bedb0da4e64c3}\label{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a5cd5238acd84dd259f3bedb0da4e64c3}} 
\index{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}!unregister\+\_\+\+Solver@{unregister\+\_\+\+Solver}}
\index{unregister\+\_\+\+Solver@{unregister\+\_\+\+Solver}!S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block@{S\+M\+Spp\+\_\+di\+\_\+unipi\+\_\+it\+::\+Block}}
\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{unregister\+\_\+\+Solver()}{unregister\_Solver()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [2/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily virtual void unregister\+\_\+\+Solver (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespace_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_a8d60e91b37db595a92964f6f4a52bf17}{c\+\_\+\+Lst\+\_\+\+Solver\+\_\+it}}}]{it }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [virtual]}}



removing the solver in position it of the set of the registered ones 

Method for removing a \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} from the set of those currently registered with the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}}. The parameter it is supposed to be the position into the list returned by get\+\_\+regisgtered\+\_\+solvers() \mbox{[}see above\mbox{]} where the \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} currently is; the vector of registered \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_solver}{Solver}} is therefore shortened by one, and the remaining solvers (if any) are shifted in the obvious way. Note that the iterator parameter is const because the only place where it might have been taken from (except if the method is called from another \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block}{Block}} method) is \mbox{\hyperlink{class_s_m_spp__di__unipi__it_1_1_block_a052e250efe81b0840361e70c520bdd7f}{get\+\_\+registered\+\_\+solvers()}}; however, we \char`\"{}cast away const-\/ness\char`\"{} inside. Also, note that the method is virtual because derived classes may have to do more.

Warning\+: checking if an iterator really belongs to a list is complicated, hence the method does not try to do that; clearly, calling the method with something that is not an iterator of that list results in indefinite behaviour.
